   29: function formatDate(value: Date | null): string {
   30:   if (!value) return "No due date";
   31: 
   32:   return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-GB", {
   33:     day: "numeric",
   34:     month: "short",
   35:     year: "numeric",
   36:   }).format(value);
   37: }
   38: 
   39: function getExamStatus(
   40:   assignment: UnifiedAssignment,
   41: ): "not_started" | "in_progress" | "submitted" | "marked" {
   42:   if (assignment.status === "marked") {
   43:     return "marked";
   44:   }
   45: 
   46:   if (
   47:     assignment.status === "submitted" ||
   48:     assignment.status === "marking"
   49:   ) {
   50:     return "submitted";
   51:   }
   52: 
   53:   if (assignment.status === "in_progress") {
   54:     return "in_progress";
   55:   }
   56: 
   57:   return "not_started";
   58: }
   59: 
   60: function statusLabel(
   61:   assignment: UnifiedAssignment,
   62: ): string {
   63:   switch (getExamStatus(assignment)) {
   64:     case "marked":
   65:       return "Marked";
   66:     case "submitted":
   67:       return assignment.status === "marking"
   68:         ? "Marking"
   69:         : "Submitted";
   70:     case "in_progress":
   71:       return "In progress";
   72:     default:
   73:       return "Not started";
   74:   }
   75: }
   76: 
   77: function actionLabel(
   78:   assignment: UnifiedAssignment,
   79: ): string {
   80:   switch (getExamStatus(assignment)) {
   81:     case "marked":
   82:       return "Review result";
   83:     case "submitted":
   84:       return "Review submission";
   85:     case "in_progress":
   86:       return "Resume Exam Mode";
   87:     default:
   88:       return "Enter Exam Mode";
   89:   }
   90: }
   91: 
   92: function StatusBadge({
   93:   assignment,
   94: }: {
   95:   assignment: UnifiedAssignment;
   96: }) {
   97:   const status = getExamStatus(assignment);
   98: 
   99:   if (status === "marked") {
  100:     return (
  101:       <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full bg-emerald-100 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-black text-emerald-700">
  102:         <CheckCircle2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
  103:         Marked
  104:       </span>
  105:     );
  106:   }
  107: 
  108:   if (status === "submitted") {
  109:     return (
  110:       <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full bg-cyan-100 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-black text-cyan-700">
  111:         <FileCheck2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
  112:         {assignment.status === "marking"
  113:           ? "Marking"
  114:           : "Submitted"}
  115:       </span>
  116:     );
  117:   }
  118: 
  119:   if (status === "in_progress") {
  120:     return (
  121:       <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full bg-amber-100 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-black text-amber-700">
  122:         <Clock3 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
  123:         In progress
  124:       </span>
  125:     );
  126:   }
  127: 
  128:   return (

  130:       <FileText className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />

  156: export default function StudentExamModePage() {

  162:   const [error, setError] = useState("");
  163:   const [filter, setFilter] =
  164:     useState<ExamFilter>("all");
  165: 
  166:   useEffect(() => {
  167:     let cancelled = false;
  168: 
  169:     async function load() {
  170:       if (authLoading) return;
  171: 
  172:       if (!user?.uid) {
  173:         setLoading(false);
  174:         return;
  175:       }
  176: 
  177:       try {
  178:         setLoading(true);
  179:         setError("");
  180: 
  181:         const allAssignments =
  182:           await getUnifiedStudentAssignments(
  183:             user.uid,
  184:           );
  185: 
  186:         if (!cancelled) {
  187:           setAssignments(
  188:             allAssignments.filter(
  189:               (assignment) =>
  190:                 assignment.kind === "exam",
  191:             ),
  192:           );
  193:         }
  194:       } catch (caughtError) {
  195:         console.error(
  196:           "Unable to load written exams:",
  197:           caughtError,
  198:         );
  199: 
  200:         if (!cancelled) {
  201:           setAssignments([]);
  202:           setError(
  203:             "Your written exams could not be loaded.",

  206:       } finally {
  207:         if (!cancelled) {
  208:           setLoading(false);

  210:       }
  211:     }
  212: 
  213:     void load();
  214: 
  215:     return () => {
  216:       cancelled = true;
  217:     };
  218:   }, [authLoading, user?.uid]);
  219: 
  220:   const counts = useMemo(() => {

FILE: app\learn\[topicId]\page.tsx
==============================================================================================================
    1: import { notFound } from "next/navigation";
    2: 
    3: import LessonRenderer from "@/components/lesson/LessonRenderer";
    4: import LessonNavigation from "@/components/lesson/LessonNavigation";
    5: import { topicLibrary } from "@/data/curriculum/topics";
    6: import type { Lesson, Topic } from "@/types/curriculum";
    7: 
    8: type Props = {
    9:   params: Promise<{
   10:     topicId: string;
   11:   }>;
   12: 
   13:   searchParams: Promise<{
   14:     lesson?: string;
   15:   }>;
   16: };
   17: 
   18: export default async function LessonPage({ params, searchParams }: Props) {
   19:   const { topicId } = await params;
   20:   const { lesson: requestedLessonId } = await searchParams;
   21: 
   22:   const topic = (topicLibrary as Record<string, Topic>)[topicId];
   23: 
   24:   if (!topic) {
   25:     notFound();
   26:   }
   27: 
   28:   const lessonIndex = requestedLessonId
   29:     ? topic.lessons.findIndex((item: Lesson) => item.id === requestedLessonId)
   30:     : 0;
   31: 
   32:   if (lessonIndex === -1) {
   33:     notFound();
   34:   }
   35: 
   36:   const currentLesson = topic.lessons[lessonIndex];
   37:   const previousLesson = topic.lessons[lessonIndex - 1];
   38:   const nextLesson = topic.lessons[lessonIndex + 1];
   39: 
   40:   if (!currentLesson) {
   41:     notFound();
   42:   }
   43: 
   44:   return (
   45:     <div className="space-y-8">
   46:       <LessonNavigation
   47:         topicId={topicId}
   48:         currentIndex={lessonIndex}
   49:         totalLessons={topic.lessons.length}
   50:         previousLessonId={previousLesson?.id}
   51:         nextLessonId={nextLesson?.id}
   52:       />
   53: 
   54:       <LessonRenderer
   55:         lesson={currentLesson}
   56:         topicId={topicId}
   57:         nextLessonId={nextLesson?.id}
   58:         topicSimulator={topic.simulator}
   59:       />
   60:     </div>
   61:   );
   62: }

  371:                   })}
  372:                 </div>
  373:               )}
  374:             </section>
  375:           );
  376:         },
  377:       )}
  378:     </div>
  379:   );
  380: }
  381: 
  382: function Metric({
  383:   label,
  384:   value,
  385:   className,
  386: }: {
  387:   label: string;
  388:   value: number;
  389:   className: string;
  390: }) {
  391:   return (
  392:     <div
  393:       className={`rounded-xl px-4 py-3 ${className}`}
  394:     >
  395:       <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase opacity-70">
  396:         {label}
  397:       </p>
  398: 
  399:       <p className="mt-1 text-2xl font-black">
  400:         {value}
  401:       </p>
  402:     </div>
  403:   );
  404: }

FILE: app\quiz\page.tsx
==============================================================================================================
    1: "use client";
    2: 
    3: import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
    4: import {
    5:   useRouter,
    6:   useSearchParams,
    7: } from "next/navigation";
    8: import {
    9:   doc,
   10:   getDoc,
   11: } from "firebase/firestore";
   12: 
   13: import QuizPlayer from "@/components/quiz/QuizPlayer";
   14: import Card from "@/components/ui/Card";
   15: import Button from "@/components/ui/Button";
   16: import Skeleton from "@/components/ui/Skeleton";
   17: 
   18: import { useAuth } from "@/contexts/AuthContext";
   19: 
   20: import { curriculumRegistry } from "@/data/curriculum/curriculumRegistry";
   21: 
   22: import {
   23:   getCurriculumQuizByTopic,
   24:   getCurriculumQuizzes,
   25:   getCurriculumTopicsWithoutQuizzes,
   26: } from "@/data/quizzes/quizRegistry";
   27: 
   28: import { db } from "@/lib/firebase";
   29: 
   30: import type { Quiz } from "@/types/quiz";
   31: import type {
   32:   ExamBoard,
   33:   Qualification,
   34: } from "@/types/user";
   35: 
   36: type SavedQuizQuestion = {
   37:   id?: string;
   38:   type?: string;
   39:   question?: string;
   40:   options?: string[];
   41:   correctAnswer?: string;
   42:   explanation?: string;
   43:   xpReward?: number;
   44: };
   45: 
   46: function normaliseLookupValue(
   47:   value: string,
   48: ): string {
   49:   return value
   50:     .trim()
   51:     .toLowerCase()
   52:     .replace(/&/g, "and")
   53:     .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, " ")
   54:     .trim();
   55: }
   56: 
   57: /*
   58:  * Query strings may contain either:
   59:  *
   60:  *   /quiz?topic=binary
   61:  *
   62:  * or a human-readable adaptive-learning topic:
   63:  *
   64:  *   /quiz?topic=Binary%20Numbers
   65:  *
   66:  * Resolve both forms through the canonical curriculum registry.
   67:  */
   68: function resolveCurriculumTopicId(
   69:   value: string,
   70: ): string | null {
   71:   const cleaned =
   72:     normaliseLookupValue(value);
   73: 
   74:   if (!cleaned) {
   75:     return null;
   76:   }
   77: 
   78:   const match =
   79:     curriculumRegistry.find(
   80:       (topic) => {
   81:         const searchable = [
   82:           topic.id,
   83:           topic.title,
   84:           ...(topic.aliases || []),
   85:         ].map(
   86:           normaliseLookupValue,
   87:         );
   88: 
   89:         return searchable.includes(
   90:           cleaned,
   91:         );
   92:       },
   93:     );
   94: 
   95:   return match?.id || null;
   96: }
   97: 
   98: export default function QuizPage() {
   99:   const router = useRouter();
  100:   const searchParams =
  101:     useSearchParams();
  102: 
  103:   const {
  104:     profile,
  105:     loading: authLoading,
  106:     profileReady,
  107:   } = useAuth();
  108: 
  109:   const topicParam =
  110:     searchParams.get("topic");
  111: 
  112:   const assignmentId =
  113:     searchParams.get(
  114:       "assignment",
  115:     );
  116: 
  117:   const [
  118:     selectedQuizId,
  119:     setSelectedQuizId,
  120:   ] = useState<
  121:     string | null
  122:   >(null);
  123: 
  124:   const [

  126:     setGeneratedQuiz,
  127:   ] = useState<Quiz | null>(
  128:     null,
  129:   );
  130: 
  131:   const [

  135:

  140:

  206:

  210:

  214:       !profileReady

  218:

  239:       resolveCurriculumTopicId(
  240:         topicParam,
  241:       );

  245:       qualification &&

  252:           examBoard,

  283:       false;
  284: 
  285:     async function loadGeneratedQuiz() {
  286:       setLoadingGeneratedQuiz(

  288:       );

  314:             null,
  315:           );
  316:

  332:             );
  333:           }
  334:

  336:         }

  350:           rawQuestions

  352:               (question) =>
  353:                 typeof question.question ===
  354:                   "string" &&
  355:                 Array.isArray(
  356:                   question.options,
  357:                 ) &&
  358:                 typeof question.correctAnswer ===
  359:                   "string",
  360:             )
  361:             .map(
  362:               (
  363:                 question,
  364:                 index,
  365:               ) => ({
  366:                 id:

  368:                   `${quizSnapshot.id}-question-${index + 1}`,

  371:                   "multipleChoice" as const,

  373:                 question:
  374:                   question.question ||
  375:                   `Question ${index + 1}`,

  378:                   question.options ||

  380:

  382:                   question.correctAnswer ||

  384:

  386:                   question.explanation ||
  387:                   "Review this topic with your teacher.",
  388:

  390:                   typeof question.xpReward ===
  391:                   "number"
  392:                     ? question.xpReward

  394:               }),

  399:           0

  402:             null,
  403:           );

  407:           );

  413:           Quiz = {
  414:           id:
  415:             quizSnapshot.id,
  416: 
  417:           /*
  418:            * Prefer stored curriculum metadata if the generated
  419:            * quiz has it. Fall back to the document ID for legacy

  421:            */

  423:             typeof data.topicId ===
  424:               "string" &&
  425:             data.topicId.trim()
  426:               ? data.topicId.trim()

  431:               "string" &&

  442:

  448:               : "10 minutes",

  451:         };
  452:

  454:           loadedQuiz,
  455:         );
  456:       } catch (error) {

  458:           "Failed to load generated quiz:",
  459:           error,
  460:         );
  461: 
  462:         if (!cancelled) {
  463:           setGeneratedQuiz(
  464:             null,
  465:           );
  466: 
  467:           setLoadError(
  468:             "The assigned quiz could not be loaded.",
  469:           );
  470:         }
  471:       } finally {
  472:         if (!cancelled) {
  473:           setLoadingGeneratedQuiz(
  474:             false,
  475:           );
  476:         }
  477:       }
  478:     }
  479: 
  480:     void loadGeneratedQuiz();
  481: 
  482:     return () => {
  483:       cancelled = true;
  484:     };
  485:   }, [
  486:     topicParam,
  487:     qualification,
  488:     examBoard,
  489:     authLoading,
  490:     profileReady,
  491:   ]);

   16:   saveAssignmentResult,
   17:   type QuizIntegrityIncident,
   18: } from "@/services/assignmentResultService";

   20: 
   21: type Props = {
   22:   quiz: Quiz;
   23: };
   24: 
   25: type QuizDeliveryMode = "practice" | "assessment";
   26: 
   27: type LinkedAssignment = {
   28:   id: string;
   29:   classId: string;
   30:   teacherId: string;
   31:   resourceId: string;
   32:   deliveryMode: QuizDeliveryMode;
   33: };
   34: 
   35: const DEFAULT_QUIZ_DURATION_SECONDS = 8 * 60;
   36: const FULLSCREEN_EXIT_COUNTDOWN_SECONDS = 5;
   37: 
   38: function parseQuizDurationSeconds(value: string): number {
   39:   const normalised = value.trim().toLowerCase();
   40: 
   41:   if (!normalised) {
   42:     return DEFAULT_QUIZ_DURATION_SECONDS;
   43:   }
   44: 
   45:   const hoursMatch = normalised.match(
   46:     /(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*(?:hours?|hrs?|hr|h)\b/,
   47:   );
   48: 
   49:   const minutesMatch = normalised.match(
   50:     /(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*(?:minutes?|mins?|min|m)\b/,
   51:   );
   52: 
   53:   const secondsMatch = normalised.match(
   54:     /(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*(?:seconds?|secs?|sec|s)\b/,
   55:   );
   56: 
   57:   const hours = hoursMatch ? Number(hoursMatch[1]) : 0;
   58:   const minutes = minutesMatch ? Number(minutesMatch[1]) : 0;
   59:   const seconds = secondsMatch ? Number(secondsMatch[1]) : 0;
   60: 
   61:   const totalSeconds = Math.round(
   62:     hours * 60 * 60 +
   63:       minutes * 60 +
   64:       seconds,
   65:   );
   66: 
   67:   return totalSeconds > 0
   68:     ? totalSeconds
   69:     : DEFAULT_QUIZ_DURATION_SECONDS;
   70: }
   71: 
   72: function getGrade(scorePercent: number) {
   73:   if (scorePercent >= 90) return "Grade 9";
   74:   if (scorePercent >= 80) return "Grade 8";
   75:   if (scorePercent >= 70) return "Grade 7";
   76:   if (scorePercent >= 60) return "Grade 6";
   77:   if (scorePercent >= 50) return "Grade 5";
   78:   if (scorePercent >= 40) return "Grade 4";
   79:   if (scorePercent >= 30) return "Grade 3";
   80:   if (scorePercent >= 20) return "Grade 2";
   81:   return "Grade 1";
   82: }
   83: 
   84: function getMessage(scorePercent: number) {
   85:   if (scorePercent >= 90) return "Outstanding performance!";
   86:   if (scorePercent >= 75) return "Excellent work!";
   87:   if (scorePercent >= 60) return "Good progress!";
   88:   if (scorePercent >= 40) return "You are getting there.";
   89:   return "Keep practising. You can improve this.";
   90: }
   91: 
   92: function createIncident(
   93:   type: QuizIntegrityIncident["type"],
   94:   questionNumber: number | null,
   95:   detail: string,
   96: ): QuizIntegrityIncident {
   97:   return {
   98:     id: `${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 9)}`,
   99:     type,
  100:     occurredAt: new Date().toISOString(),
  101:     questionNumber,
  102:     detail,
  103:   };
  104: }
  105: 
  106: export default function QuizPlayer({ quiz }: Props) {
  107:   const { user } = useAuth();
  108:   const searchParams = useSearchParams();
  109: 
  110:   const assignmentId = searchParams.get("assignment");
  111: 
  112:   const quizDurationSeconds = useMemo(
  113:     () => parseQuizDurationSeconds(quiz.estimatedTime),
  114:     [quiz.estimatedTime],
  115:   );
  116: 
  117:   const assessmentRootRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);
  118:   const quizSaveInProgress = useRef(false);
  119:   const assignmentSaveInProgress = useRef(false);
  120:   const finishingRef = useRef(false);
  121: 
  122:   /*
  123:    * Synchronous integrity termination flag.
  124:    *
  125:    * React state updates are asynchronous. This ref makes the zero-XP rule
  126:    * available immediately to save operations when auto-submit occurs.
  127:    */
  128:   const integrityTerminatedRef = useRef(false);
  129: 
  130:   const fullscreenExitActiveRef = useRef(false);
  131:   const countdownTimerRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null>(null);
  132:   const integrityIncidentsRef = useRef<QuizIntegrityIncident[]>([]);
  133: 
  134:   const [currentIndex, setCurrentIndex] = useState(0);
  135:   const [answers, setAnswers] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  136:   const [showResults, setShowResults] = useState(false);
  137:   const [timeLeft, setTimeLeft] = useState(() => quizDurationSeconds);
  138:   const [resultSaved, setResultSaved] = useState(false);
  139:   const [assignmentResultSaved, setAssignmentResultSaved] = useState(false);
  140: 
  141:   const [linkedAssignment, setLinkedAssignment] =
  142:     useState<LinkedAssignment | null>(null);
  143:   const [assignmentLoading, setAssignmentLoading] = useState(Boolean(assignmentId));
  144:   const [assignmentError, setAssignmentError] = useState("");
  145: 
  146:   const [integrityStarted, setIntegrityStarted] = useState(false);
  147:   const [integritySessionStartedAt, setIntegritySessionStartedAt] =
  148:     useState<string | null>(null);
  149:   const [fullscreenCountdown, setFullscreenCountdown] =
  150:     useState<number | null>(null);
  151:   const [integrityWarning, setIntegrityWarning] = useState("");
  152:   const [integrityTerminated, setIntegrityTerminated] = useState(false);
  153:   const [integrityTerminationReason, setIntegrityTerminationReason] =
  154:     useState("");
  155: 
  156:   const deliveryMode: QuizDeliveryMode =
  157:     linkedAssignment?.deliveryMode ?? "practice";
  158:   const assessmentMode =
  159:     Boolean(assignmentId) && deliveryMode === "assessment";
  160: 
  161:   const currentQuestion = quiz.questions[currentIndex];
  162:   const selectedAnswer = currentQuestion
  163:     ? answers[currentQuestion.id] || ""
  164:     : "";
  165: 
  166:   const currentQuestionNumber = currentIndex + 1;
  167: 
  168:   const progress = Math.round(
  169:     ((currentIndex + 1) / Math.max(1, quiz.questions.length)) * 100,
  170:   );
  171: 
  172:   const totalXP = useMemo(() => {
  173:     return quiz.questions.reduce(
  174:       (total, question) => total + question.xpReward,
  175:       0,
  176:     );
  177:   }, [quiz.questions]);
  178: 
  179:   const correctCount = quiz.questions.filter((question) => {
  180:     const userAnswer = answers[question.id]?.trim().toLowerCase();
  181:     const correctAnswer = question.correctAnswer.trim().toLowerCase();
  182:     return userAnswer === correctAnswer;
  183:   }).length;
  184: 
  185:   const scorePercent = Math.round(
  186:     (correctCount / Math.max(1, quiz.questions.length)) * 100,
  187:   );
  188: 
  189:   const earnedXP = quiz.questions.reduce((total, question) => {
  190:     const userAnswer = answers[question.id]?.trim().toLowerCase();
  191:     const correctAnswer = question.correctAnswer.trim().toLowerCase();
  192: 
  193:     return userAnswer === correctAnswer
  194:       ? total + question.xpReward
  195:       : total;
  196:   }, 0);
  197: 
  198:   /*
  199:    * Integrity-terminated assessment attempts retain their raw score for
  200:    * teacher evidence, but do not award XP.
  201:    */
  202:   const awardedXP = integrityTerminated ? 0 : earnedXP;
  203: 
  204:   const strengths = quiz.questions
  205:     .filter((question) => {
  206:       const userAnswer = answers[question.id]?.trim().toLowerCase();
  207:       const correctAnswer = question.correctAnswer.trim().toLowerCase();
  208:       return userAnswer === correctAnswer;
  209:     })
  210:     .slice(0, 3);
  211: 
  212:   const needsPractice = quiz.questions
  213:     .filter((question) => {
  214:       const userAnswer = answers[question.id]?.trim().toLowerCase();
  215:       const correctAnswer = question.correctAnswer.trim().toLowerCase();
  216:       return userAnswer !== correctAnswer;
  217:     })
  218:     .slice(0, 3);
  219: 
  220:   useEffect(() => {
  221:     let cancelled = false;
  222: 
  223:     async function loadLinkedAssignment() {
  224:       if (!assignmentId) {
  225:         setLinkedAssignment(null);
  226:         setAssignmentLoading(false);
  227:         return;
  228:       }
  229: 
  230:       if (!user?.uid) {
  231:         setLinkedAssignment(null);
  232:         setAssignmentError(
  233:           "Sign in with the student account that received this assignment.",
  234:         );
  235:         setAssignmentLoading(false);
  236:         return;
  237:       }
  238: 
  239:       try {
  240:         setAssignmentLoading(true);
  241:         setAssignmentError("");
  242: 
  243:         const snapshot = await getDoc(
  244:           doc(db, "assignments", assignmentId),
  245:         );
  246: 
  247:         if (!snapshot.exists()) {
  248:           throw new Error(
  249:             "The linked quiz assignment could not be found.",
  250:           );
  251:         }
  252: 
  253:         const data = snapshot.data();
  254: 
  255:         if (data.type !== "quiz") {
  256:           throw new Error(
  257:             "This assignment is not a quiz assignment.",
  258:           );
  259:         }
  260: 
  261:         if (!data.classId || !data.teacherId) {
  262:           throw new Error(
  263:             "The assignment is missing its class or teacher information.",
  264:           );
  265:         }
  266: 
  267:         if (data.status === "cancelled") {
  268:           throw new Error(
  269:             "This quiz assignment has been cancelled.",
  270:           );
  271:         }
  272: 
  273:         const assignedResourceId =
  274:           typeof data.resourceId === "string"
  275:             ? data.resourceId.trim()
  276:             : "";
  277: 
  278:         if (
  279:           !assignedResourceId ||
  280:           ![
  281:             quiz.id,
  282:             quiz.topicId,
  283:           ].includes(assignedResourceId)
  284:         ) {
  285:           throw new Error(
  286:             "This quiz does not match the linked assignment.",
  287:           );
  288:         }
  289: 
  290:         const classSnapshot = await getDoc(
  291:           doc(
  292:             db,
  293:             "classes",
  294:             data.classId,
  295:           ),
  296:         );
  297: 
  298:         if (!classSnapshot.exists()) {
  299:           throw new Error(
  300:             "The class linked to this assignment could not be found.",
  301:           );
  302:         }
  303: 
  304:         const classData =
  305:           classSnapshot.data();
  306: 
  307:         const studentIds =
  308:           Array.isArray(
  309:             classData.studentIds,
  310:           )
  311:             ? classData.studentIds.filter(
  312:                 (
  313:                   value,
  314:                 ): value is string =>
  315:                   typeof value ===
  316:                     "string" &&
  317:                   Boolean(
  318:                     value.trim(),
  319:                   ),
  320:               )
  321:             : [];
  322: 
  323:         if (
  324:           !studentIds.includes(
  325:             user.uid,
  326:           )
  327:         ) {
  328:           throw new Error(
  329:             "You do not have access to this quiz assignment.",
  330:           );
  331:         }
  332: 
  333:         if (!cancelled) {
  334:           setLinkedAssignment({
  335:             id: snapshot.id,
  336:             classId: data.classId,
  337:             teacherId: data.teacherId,
  338:             resourceId:
  339:               assignedResourceId,
  340:             deliveryMode:
  341:               data.deliveryMode ===
  342:               "assessment"
  343:                 ? "assessment"
  344:                 : "practice",
  345:           });
  346:         }
  347:       } catch (error) {
  348:         console.error(
  349:           "Quiz assignment load error:",
  350:           error,
  351:         );
  352: 
  353:         if (!cancelled) {
  354:           setLinkedAssignment(null);
  355:           setAssignmentError(
  356:             error instanceof Error
  357:               ? error.message
  358:               : "The linked quiz assignment could not be loaded.",
  359:           );
  360:         }
  361:       } finally {
  362:         if (!cancelled) {
  363:           setAssignmentLoading(false);
  364:         }
  365:       }
  366:     }
  367: 
  368:     void loadLinkedAssignment();
  369: 
  370:     return () => {
  371:       cancelled = true;
  372:     };
  373:   }, [
  374:     assignmentId,
  375:     quiz.id,
  376:     quiz.topicId,
  377:     user?.uid,
  378:   ]);
  379: 
  380:   useEffect(() => {
  381:     void Promise.resolve().then(() => {
  382:       setTimeLeft(quizDurationSeconds);
  383:     });
  384:   }, [quiz.id, quizDurationSeconds]);
  385: 
  386:   useEffect(() => {
  387:     return () => {
  388:       if (countdownTimerRef.current) {
  389:         clearInterval(countdownTimerRef.current);
  390:       }
  391:     };
  392:   }, []);
  393: 
  394:   function appendIntegrityIncident(
  395:     type: QuizIntegrityIncident["type"],
  396:     detail: string,
  397:   ) {
  398:     const incident = createIncident(
  399:       type,
  400:       currentQuestionNumber,
  401:       detail,
  402:     );
  403: 
  404:     integrityIncidentsRef.current = [
  405:       ...integrityIncidentsRef.current,
  406:       incident,
  407:     ];
  408:   }
  409: 
  410:   async function leaveFullscreenSafely() {
  411:     if (document.fullscreenElement) {
  412:       await document.exitFullscreen().catch(() => undefined);
  413:     }
  414:   }
  415: 
  416:   async function finishQuiz({
  417:     terminated = false,
  418:     reason = "",
  419:   }: {
  420:     terminated?: boolean;
  421:     reason?: string;
  422:   } = {}) {
  423:     if (finishingRef.current || showResults) {
  424:       return;
  425:     }
  426: 
  427:     finishingRef.current = true;
  428: 
  429:     if (countdownTimerRef.current) {
  430:       clearInterval(countdownTimerRef.current);
  431:       countdownTimerRef.current = null;
  432:     }
  433: 
  434:     fullscreenExitActiveRef.current = false;
  435:     setFullscreenCountdown(null);
  436: 
  437:     if (terminated) {
  438:       /*
  439:        * Set the ref first so save effects see termination synchronously,
  440:        * before React finishes applying the state update.
  441:        */
  442:       integrityTerminatedRef.current = true;
  443:       setIntegrityTerminated(true);
  444:       setIntegrityTerminationReason(reason);
  445: 
  446:       appendIntegrityIncident(
  447:         "auto_submit",
  448:         reason || "The assessment was automatically submitted.",
  449:       );
  450:     }
  451: 
  452:     await leaveFullscreenSafely();
  453:     setShowResults(true);
  454:   }
  455: 
  456:   const finishQuizFromTimer = useEffectEvent(
  457:     (options: { terminated?: boolean; reason?: string }) => {
  458:       void finishQuiz(options);
  459:     },
  460:   );
  461: 
  462:   useEffect(() => {
  463:     if (
  464:       showResults ||
  465:       assignmentLoading ||
  466:       (assessmentMode && !integrityStarted)
  467:     ) {
  468:       return;
  469:     }
  470: 
  471:     const timer = window.setInterval(() => {
  472:       setTimeLeft((previousTime) => {
  473:         if (previousTime <= 1) {
  474:           window.clearInterval(timer);
  475: 
  476:           finishQuizFromTimer({
  477:             terminated: assessmentMode,
  478:             reason: assessmentMode
  479:               ? "The assessment timer expired and the quiz was automatically submitted."
  480:               : "",
  481:           });
  482: 
  483:           return 0;
  484:         }
  485: 
  486:         return previousTime - 1;
  487:       });
  488:     }, 1000);
  489: 
  490:     return () => window.clearInterval(timer);
  491:   }, [
  492:     showResults,
  493:     assignmentLoading,
  494:     assessmentMode,
  495:     integrityStarted,
  496:   ]);
  497: 
  498:   function beginFullscreenCountdown() {
  499:     if (
  500:       !assessmentMode ||
  501:       !integrityStarted ||
  502:       showResults ||
  503:       finishingRef.current ||
  504:       fullscreenExitActiveRef.current
  505:     ) {
  506:       return;
  507:     }
  508: 
  509:     fullscreenExitActiveRef.current = true;
  510: 
  511:     setFullscreenCountdown(FULLSCREEN_EXIT_COUNTDOWN_SECONDS);
  512: 
  513:     appendIntegrityIncident(
  514:       "fullscreen_exit",
  515:       `Fullscreen was exited. A ${FULLSCREEN_EXIT_COUNTDOWN_SECONDS}-second return countdown started.`,
  516:     );
  517: 
  518:     let remaining = FULLSCREEN_EXIT_COUNTDOWN_SECONDS;
  519: 
  520:     countdownTimerRef.current = setInterval(() => {
  521:       remaining -= 1;
  522:       setFullscreenCountdown(Math.max(remaining, 0));
  523: 
  524:       if (remaining <= 0) {
  525:         if (countdownTimerRef.current) {
  526:           clearInterval(countdownTimerRef.current);
  527:           countdownTimerRef.current = null;
  528:         }
  529: 
  530:         void finishQuiz({
  531:           terminated: true,
  532:           reason:
  533:             "The learner exited fullscreen and did not return within 5 seconds.",
  534:         });
  535:       }
  536:     }, 1000);
  537:   }
  538: 
  539:   function resolveFullscreenExit() {
  540:     if (!fullscreenExitActiveRef.current) {
  541:       return;
  542:     }
  543: 
  544:     fullscreenExitActiveRef.current = false;
  545: 
  546:     if (countdownTimerRef.current) {
  547:       clearInterval(countdownTimerRef.current);
  548:       countdownTimerRef.current = null;
  549:     }
  550: 
  551:     setFullscreenCountdown(null);
  552: 
  553:     appendIntegrityIncident(
  554:       "fullscreen_restored",
  555:       "Fullscreen was restored before the five-second termination countdown expired.",
  556:     );
  557:   }
  558: 
  559:   const handleFullscreenChange = useEffectEvent(() => {
  560:     if (finishingRef.current) {
  561:       return;
  562:     }
  563: 
  564:     if (
  565:       document.fullscreenElement ===
  566:       assessmentRootRef.current
  567:     ) {
  568:       resolveFullscreenExit();
  569:     } else {
  570:       beginFullscreenCountdown();
  571:     }
  572:   });
  573: 
  574:   const handleVisibilityChange = useEffectEvent(() => {
  575:     if (finishingRef.current) {
  576:       return;
  577:     }
  578: 
  579:     if (document.visibilityState === "hidden") {
  580:       appendIntegrityIncident(
  581:         "page_hidden",
  582:         "The assessment page became hidden.",
  583:       );
  584: 
  585:       setIntegrityWarning(
  586:         "The assessment page was hidden. This incident has been recorded.",
  587:       );
  588:     } else {
  589:       appendIntegrityIncident(
  590:         "page_visible",
  591:         "The assessment page became visible again.",
  592:       );
  593:     }
  594:   });
  595: 
  596:   useEffect(() => {
  597:     if (
  598:       !assessmentMode ||
  599:       !integrityStarted ||
  600:       showResults
  601:     ) {
  602:       return;
  603:     }
  604: 
  605:     function onFullscreenChange() {
  606:       handleFullscreenChange();
  607:     }
  608: 
  609:     function onVisibilityChange() {
  610:       handleVisibilityChange();
  611:     }
  612: 
  613:     document.addEventListener(
  614:       "fullscreenchange",
  615:       onFullscreenChange,
  616:     );
  617:     document.addEventListener(
  618:       "visibilitychange",
  619:       onVisibilityChange,
  620:     );
  621: 
  622:     return () => {
  623:       document.removeEventListener(
  624:         "fullscreenchange",
  625:         onFullscreenChange,
  626:       );
  627:       document.removeEventListener(
  628:         "visibilitychange",
  629:         onVisibilityChange,
  630:       );
  631:     };
  632:   }, [
  633:     assessmentMode,
  634:     integrityStarted,
  635:     showResults,
  636:   ]);
  637: 
  638:   async function enterAssessmentMode() {
  639:     if (!assessmentRootRef.current) {
  640:       return;
  641:     }
  642: 
  643:     if (!document.fullscreenEnabled) {
  644:       toast.error(
  645:         "Fullscreen is not available in this browser. Ask your teacher for support.",
  646:       );
  647:       return;
  648:     }
  649: 
  650:     try {
  651:       await assessmentRootRef.current.requestFullscreen();
  652: 
  653:       const startedAt = new Date().toISOString();
  654:       setIntegritySessionStartedAt(startedAt);
  655:       setIntegrityStarted(true);
  656:       setIntegrityWarning("");
  657:       integrityTerminatedRef.current = false;
  658:       setIntegrityTerminated(false);
  659:       setIntegrityTerminationReason("");
  660:       finishingRef.current = false;
  661:       integrityIncidentsRef.current = [];
  662: 
  663:       toast.success("Monitored assessment started.");
  664:     } catch (error) {
  665:       console.error(
  666:         "Unable to enter monitored assessment mode:",
  667:         error,
  668:       );
  669: 
  670:       toast.error(
  671:         "Fullscreen assessment mode could not be started.",
  672:       );
  673:     }
  674:   }
  675: 
  676:   async function returnToFullscreen() {
  677:     if (!assessmentRootRef.current) {
  678:       return;
  679:     }
  680: 
  681:     try {
  682:       await assessmentRootRef.current.requestFullscreen();
  683:     } catch {
  684:       toast.error(
  685:         "Fullscreen could not be restored. Return before the countdown reaches zero.",
  686:       );
  687:     }
  688:   }
  689: 
  690:   useEffect(() => {
  691:     async function saveNormalQuizResult() {
  692:       if (
  693:         !showResults ||
  694:         resultSaved ||
  695:         !user ||
  696:         quizSaveInProgress.current
  697:       ) {
  698:         return;
  699:       }
  700: 
  701:       quizSaveInProgress.current = true;
  702: 
  703:       try {
  704:         await saveQuizResult({
  705:           uid: user.uid,
  706:           quizId: quiz.id,
  707:           topicId: quiz.topicId,
  708:           title: quiz.title,
  709:           scorePercent,
  710:           correctCount,
  711:           totalQuestions: quiz.questions.length,
  712:           earnedXP:
  713:             integrityTerminatedRef.current
  714:               ? 0
  715:               : awardedXP,
  716:         });
  717: 
  718:         setResultSaved(true);
  719: 
  720:         toast.success(
  721:           integrityTerminated
  722:             ? "Assessment result saved. No XP was awarded."
  723:             : `Quiz result saved! +${awardedXP} XP`,
  724:         );
  725:       } catch (error) {
  726:         console.error("Quiz save error:", error);
  727:         toast.error("Could not save quiz result.");
  728:       } finally {
  729:         quizSaveInProgress.current = false;
  730:       }
  731:     }
  732: 
  733:     void saveNormalQuizResult();
  734:   }, [
  735:     showResults,
  736:     resultSaved,
  737:     user,
  738:     quiz.id,
  739:     quiz.topicId,
  740:     quiz.title,
  741:     quiz.questions.length,
  742:     scorePercent,
  743:     correctCount,
  744:     awardedXP,
  745:     integrityTerminated,
  746:   ]);
  747: 
  748:   useEffect(() => {
  749:     async function saveLinkedAssignmentResult() {
  750:       if (
  751:         !showResults ||
  752:         !assignmentId ||
  753:         !linkedAssignment ||
  754:         assignmentResultSaved ||
  755:         !user ||
  756:         assignmentSaveInProgress.current
  757:       ) {
  758:         return;
  759:       }
  760: 
  761:       assignmentSaveInProgress.current = true;
  762: 
  763:       try {
  764:         await saveAssignmentResult({
  765:           assignmentId,
  766:           studentId: user.uid,
  767:           classId: linkedAssignment.classId,
  768:           teacherId: linkedAssignment.teacherId,
  769:           assignmentType: "quiz",
  770:           resourceId: linkedAssignment.resourceId || quiz.topicId,
  771:           score: correctCount,
  772:           totalQuestions: quiz.questions.length,
  773:           percentage: scorePercent,
  774:           earnedXP:
  775:             integrityTerminatedRef.current
  776:               ? 0
  777:               : awardedXP,
  778:           timeTakenSeconds: Math.max(0, quizDurationSeconds - timeLeft),
  779: 
  780:           ...(assessmentMode
  781:             ? {
  782:                 deliveryMode: "assessment" as const,
  783:                 integritySessionStartedAt,
  784:                 integrityIncidents: integrityIncidentsRef.current,
  785:                 integrityTerminated,
  786:                 integrityTerminationReason,
  787:               }
  788:             : {
  789:                 deliveryMode: "practice" as const,
  790:               }),
  791:         });
  792: 
  793:         setAssignmentResultSaved(true);
  794: 
  795:         toast.success(
  796:           integrityTerminated
  797:             ? "Assessment automatically submitted."
  798:             : "Assignment marked as completed.",
  799:         );
  800:       } catch (error) {
  801:         console.error("Assignment result save error:", error);
  802: 
  803:         toast.error(
  804:           error instanceof Error
  805:             ? error.message
  806:             : "Could not mark the assignment as completed.",
  807:         );
  808:       } finally {
  809:         assignmentSaveInProgress.current = false;
  810:       }
  811:     }
  812: 
  813:     void saveLinkedAssignmentResult();
  814:   }, [
  815:     showResults,
  816:     assignmentId,
  817:     linkedAssignment,
  818:     assignmentResultSaved,
  819:     user,
  820:     quiz.topicId,
  821:     quiz.questions.length,
  822:     correctCount,

  824:     awardedXP,
  825:     timeLeft,
  826:     quizDurationSeconds,
  827:     assessmentMode,
  828:     integritySessionStartedAt,
  829:     integrityTerminated,
  830:     integrityTerminationReason,
  831:   ]);
  832: 
  833:   function formatTime(seconds: number) {
  834:     const minutes = Math.floor(seconds / 60);
  835:     const remainingSeconds = seconds % 60;
  836: 
  837:     return `${minutes}:${remainingSeconds
  838:       .toString()
  839:       .padStart(2, "0")}`;
  840:   }
  841: 
  842:   function saveAnswer(answer: string) {
  843:     if (assessmentMode && !integrityStarted) {
  844:       return;
  845:     }
  846: 
  847:     setAnswers((previousAnswers) => ({
  848:       ...previousAnswers,
  849:       [currentQuestion.id]: answer,
  850:     }));
  851:   }
  852: 
  853:   function goNext() {
  854:     if (currentIndex < quiz.questions.length - 1) {
  855:       setCurrentIndex((previousIndex) => previousIndex + 1);
  856:       setIntegrityWarning("");
  857:       return;
  858:     }
  859: 
  860:     void finishQuiz();
  861:   }
  862: 
  863:   function goPrevious() {
  864:     if (currentIndex > 0) {
  865:       setCurrentIndex((previousIndex) => previousIndex - 1);
  866:       setIntegrityWarning("");
  867:     }
  868:   }
  869:

  878:   }

 1442:       {fullscreenCountdown !== null && (
 1443:         <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[100] flex items-center justify-center bg-slate-950/90 p-6 text-white">
 1444:           <div className="w-full max-w-xl rounded-3xl border border-red-400/40 bg-slate-900 p-8 text-center shadow-2xl">
 1445:             <AlertTriangle className="mx-auto h-12 w-12 text-red-400" />
 1446: 
 1447:             <p className="mt-5 text-sm font-black uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-red-300">
 1448:               Fullscreen exited
 1449:             </p>
 1450: 
 1451:             <p className="mt-4 text-7xl font-black">
 1452:               {fullscreenCountdown}
 1453:             </p>
 1454: 
 1455:             <p className="mt-4 text-lg font-bold">
 1456:               Return to fullscreen before the countdown reaches zero.
 1457:             </p>
 1458: 
 1459:             <p className="mt-3 text-sm leading-6 text-slate-300">
 1460:               If you do not return within five seconds, the quiz will be
 1461:               automatically submitted and the incident will be recorded.
 1462:             </p>
 1463: 
 1464:             <button
 1465:               type="button"
 1466:               onClick={() => void returnToFullscreen()}
 1467:               className="mt-7 w-full rounded-2xl bg-indigo-600 px-6 py-4 font-black text-white hover:bg-indigo-500"
 1468:             >
 1469:               Return to fullscreen
 1470:             </button>
 1471:           </div>
 1472:         </div>
 1473:       )}
 1474:     </div>
 1475:   );
 1476: }

  402: export function getCurriculumDefinition(
  403:   qualification: Qualification,
  404:   examBoard: ExamBoard,
  405: ): CurriculumDefinition | undefined {
  406:   return curriculumDefinitions.find(
  407:     (curriculum) =>
  408:       curriculum.qualification === qualification &&
  409:       curriculum.examBoard === examBoard,
  410:   );
  411: }

  183: export const curriculumTopicById = new Map(
  184:   curriculumRegistry.map((topic) => [topic.id, topic]),
  185: );

==============================================================================================================
FILE: data\curriculum\topicAliases.ts
==============================================================================================================
    1: import { curriculumRegistry } from "@/data/curriculum/curriculumRegistry";
    2: 
    3: export function normaliseAliasKey(value: string): string {
    4:   return value
    5:     .trim()
    6:     .toLowerCase()
    7:     .replace(/[_/\\]+/g, " ")
    8:     .replace(/[-]+/g, " ")
    9:     .replace(/\s+/g, " ");
   10: }
   11: 
   12: export const topicAliasMap = new Map<string, string>();
   13: 
   14: curriculumRegistry.forEach((topic) => {
   15:   [topic.id, topic.title, ...topic.aliases, ...topic.lessonIds].forEach(
   16:     (value) => {
   17:       topicAliasMap.set(normaliseAliasKey(value), topic.id);
   18:     },
   19:   );
   20: });

    2: 
    3: export const topicDependencies = new Map(
    4:   curriculumRegistry.map((topic) => [topic.id, topic.prerequisites]),
    5: );

   22: import { advancedProgrammingTopic } from "./a-level/advanced-programming";
   23: import { advancedSystemsTopic } from "./a-level/advanced-systems";
   24: import { bigDataTopic } from "./a-level/big-data";
   25: import { computationalThinkingTopic } from "./a-level/computational-thinking";
   26: import { dataStructuresTopic } from "./a-level/data-structures";
   27: import { functionalProgrammingTopic } from "./a-level/functional-programming";
   28: import { legalEthicalALevelTopic } from "./a-level/legal-ethical-a-level";
   29: import { softwareDevelopmentTopic } from "./a-level/software-development";
   30: import { theoryComputationTopic } from "./a-level/theory-computation";
   31: 
   32: import type { Topic } from "@/types/curriculum";
   33: 
   34: function createTopicLibrary(
   35:   topics: Topic[],
   36: ): Record<string, Topic> {
   37:   return Object.fromEntries(
   38:     topics.map((topic) => [topic.id, topic]),
   39:   );
   40: }
   41: 
   42: export const publishedTopics: Topic[] = [
   43:   binaryTopic,
   44:   hexadecimalTopic,
   45:   charactersTopic,
   46:   imagesTopic,
   47:   soundTopic,
   48:   compressionTopic,
   49:   cpuTopic,
   50:   memoryStorageTopic,
   51:   systemsSoftwareTopic,
   52:   networksTopic,
   53:   cyberSecurityTopic,
   54:   algorithmsTopic,
   55:   programmingTopic,
   56:   databasesTopic,
   57:   booleanLogicTopic,
   58:   ethicalLegalTopic,
   59:   programmingLanguagesTopic,
   60: 
   61:   advancedProgrammingTopic,
   62:   advancedDataRepresentationTopic,
   63:   advancedSystemsTopic,
   64:   advancedNetworksTopic,
   65:   advancedDatabasesTopic,
   66:   dataStructuresTopic,
   67:   computationalThinkingTopic,
   68:   theoryComputationTopic,
   69:   functionalProgrammingTopic,
   70:   softwareDevelopmentTopic,
   71:   bigDataTopic,
   72:   legalEthicalALevelTopic,
   73: ];
   74: 
   75: export const topicLibrary =
   76:   createTopicLibrary(publishedTopics);
   77: 
   78: export function getTopicById(
   79:   topicId: string,
   80: ): Topic | null {
   81:   return topicLibrary[topicId] ?? null;
   82: }
   83: 
   84: export function getPublishedTopics(): Topic[] {
   85:   return publishedTopics.filter(
   86:     (topic) =>
   87:       topic.status !== "coming-soon",
   88:   );
   89: }
   90: 
   91: export function getTotalPublishedLessons(): number {
   92:   return getPublishedTopics().reduce(
   93:     (total, topic) =>
   94:       total + topic.lessons.length,
   95:     0,
   96:   );
   97: }

==============================================================================================================
FILE: services\adaptiveLearningService.ts

    1: import {
    2:   collection,
    3:   doc,
    4:   getDoc,
    5:   getDocs,
    6:   orderBy,
    7:   query,
    8:   Timestamp,
    9:   where,
   10: } from "firebase/firestore";
   11: 
   12: import { db } from "@/lib/firebase";
   13: 
   14: import { buildTopicMastery } from "@/services/adaptiveMasteryService";
   15: import { buildAdaptiveActions } from "@/services/adaptiveRecommendationService";
   16: import { getStudentExamAssignments } from "@/services/examAssignmentService";
   17: import { getExamSubmission } from "@/services/examSubmissionService";
   18: import { getStudentInterventions } from "@/services/interventionService";
   19: import { indicativeGradeFromPercentage } from "@/services/qualificationGradeService";
   20: import { normaliseTopic } from "@/services/topicNormalisationService";
   21: import { getUserProfile } from "@/services/userService";
   22: 
   23: import type {
   24:   AdaptiveEvidence,
   25:   AdaptiveLearningPlan,
   26: } from "@/types/adaptiveLearning";
   27: 
   28: const safeString = (
   29:   value: unknown,
   30:   fallback = "",
   31: ): string =>
   32:   typeof value === "string" &&
   33:   value.trim()
   34:     ? value.trim()
   35:     : fallback;
   36: 
   37: const safeNumber = (
   38:   value: unknown,
   39: ): number =>
   40:   typeof value === "number" &&
   41:   Number.isFinite(value)
   42:     ? value
   43:     : 0;
   44: 
   45: const average = (
   46:   values: number[],
   47: ): number =>
   48:   values.length
   49:     ? Math.round(
   50:         values.reduce(
   51:           (sum, value) =>
   52:             sum + value,
   53:           0,
   54:         ) / values.length,
   55:       )
   56:     : 0;
   57: 
   58: function groupEvidence(
   59:   evidence: AdaptiveEvidence[],
   60: ) {
   61:   const groups =
   62:     new Map<
   63:       string,
   64:       AdaptiveEvidence[]
   65:     >();
   66: 
   67:   evidence.forEach((item) => {
   68:     const normalised =
   69:       normaliseTopic(item.topic);
   70: 
   71:     const existing =
   72:       groups.get(
   73:         normalised.topicTitle,
   74:       ) || [];
   75: 
   76:     groups.set(
   77:       normalised.topicTitle,
   78:       [
   79:         ...existing,
   80:         {
   81:           ...item,
   82:           topic:
   83:             normalised.topicTitle,
   84:         },
   85:       ],
   86:     );
   87:   });
   88: 
   89:   return Array.from(
   90:     groups.entries(),
   91:   ).map(
   92:     ([topic, items]) => ({
   93:       topic,
   94:       evidence: items,
   95:     }),
   96:   );
   97: }
   98: 
   99: function resolveRawTopic(
  100:   data: Record<string, unknown>,
  101:   fallback = "",
  102: ): string {
  103:   return (
  104:     safeString(
  105:       data.topicTitle,
  106:     ) ||
  107:     safeString(
  108:       data.topic,
  109:     ) ||
  110:     safeString(
  111:       data.curriculumTopic,
  112:     ) ||
  113:     safeString(
  114:       data.quizTitle,
  115:     ) ||
  116:     safeString(
  117:       data.title,
  118:       fallback,
  119:     )
  120:   );
  121: }
  122: 
  123: /*
  124:  * Generic labels describe an assessment type rather than
  125:  * an actual Computer Science curriculum topic.
  126:  *
  127:  * These must never become mastery topics.
  128:  */
  129: function isGenericAssessmentTopic(
  130:   value: string,
  131: ): boolean {
  132:   const normalised = value
  133:     .trim()
  134:     .toLowerCase()
  135:     .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, " ")
  136:     .trim();
  137: 
  138:   return [
  139:     "",
  140:     "quiz",
  141:     "assigned quiz",
  142:     "ai quiz",
  143:     "assessment",
  144:     "assigned assessment",
  145:     "practice quiz",
  146:     "exam",
  147:     "written exam",
  148:   ].includes(normalised);
  149: }
  150: 
  151: /*
  152:  * Assignment titles frequently contain presentational suffixes,
  153:  * for example:
  154:  *
  155:  * "character encoding Demo Quiz"
  156:  * "memory and storage Demo Quiz"
  157:  * "merge sort Demo Quiz"
  158:  *
  159:  * Removing those suffixes gives topicNormalisationService a much
  160:  * cleaner curriculum phrase to resolve.
  161:  */
  162: function cleanAssignmentTitle(
  163:   value: string,
  164: ): string {
  165:   return value
  166:     .trim()
  167:     .replace(
  168:       /\s+(demo\s+)?quiz$/i,
  169:       "",
  170:     )
  171:     .replace(
  172:       /\s+practice\s+quiz$/i,
  173:       "",
  174:     )
  175:     .replace(
  176:       /\s+assessment$/i,
  177:       "",
  178:     )
  179:     .trim();
  180: }
  181: 
  182: type AssignmentTopicMetadata = {
  183:   topic: string;
  184:   title: string;
  185: };
  186: 
  187: async function loadAssignmentTopicMetadata(
  188:   assignmentIds: string[],
  189: ): Promise<
  190:   Map<
  191:     string,
  192:     AssignmentTopicMetadata
  193:   >
  194: > {
  195:   const uniqueIds =
  196:     Array.from(
  197:       new Set(
  198:         assignmentIds
  199:           .map((id) =>
  200:             id.trim(),
  201:           )
  202:           .filter(Boolean),
  203:       ),
  204:     );
  205: 
  206:   const entries =
  207:     await Promise.all(
  208:       uniqueIds.map(
  209:         async (
  210:           assignmentId,
  211:         ) => {
  212:           try {
  213:             const snapshot =
  214:               await getDoc(
  215:                 doc(
  216:                   db,
  217:                   "assignments",
  218:                   assignmentId,
  219:                 ),
  220:               );
  221: 
  222:             if (
  223:               !snapshot.exists()
  224:             ) {
  225:               return [
  226:                 assignmentId,
  227:                 {
  228:                   topic: "",
  229:                   title: "",
  230:                 },
  231:               ] as const;
  232:             }
  233: 
  234:             const data =
  235:               snapshot.data();
  236: 
  237:             return [
  238:               assignmentId,
  239:               {
  240:                 topic:
  241:                   resolveRawTopic(
  242:                     data,
  243:                   ),
  244:                 title:
  245:                   safeString(
  246:                     data.title,
  247:                   ),
  248:               },
  249:             ] as const;
  250:           } catch (
  251:             caughtError
  252:           ) {
  253:             /*
  254:              * A missing/legacy assignment should not prevent the
  255:              * rest of the student's adaptive plan from loading.
  256:              */
  257:             console.warn(
  258:               `Unable to resolve adaptive topic for assignment ${assignmentId}:`,
  259:               caughtError,
  260:             );
  261: 
  262:             return [
  263:               assignmentId,
  264:               {
  265:                 topic: "",
  266:                 title: "",
  267:               },
  268:             ] as const;
  269:           }
  270:         },
  271:       ),
  272:     );
  273: 
  274:   return new Map(entries);
  275: }
  276: 
  277: function resolveAssignedQuizTopic({
  278:   resultData,
  279:   assignmentMetadata,
  280: }: {
  281:   resultData: Record<
  282:     string,
  283:     unknown
  284:   >;
  285: 
  286:   assignmentMetadata:
  287:     AssignmentTopicMetadata | null;
  288: }): string | null {
  289:   /*
  290:    * First preference:
  291:    * explicit curriculum/topic metadata stored directly on
  292:    * the result.
  293:    */
  294:   const resultTopic =
  295:     resolveRawTopic(
  296:       resultData,
  297:     );
  298: 
  299:   if (
  300:     resultTopic &&
  301:     !isGenericAssessmentTopic(
  302:       resultTopic,
  303:     )
  304:   ) {
  305:     return cleanAssignmentTitle(
  306:       resultTopic,
  307:     );
  308:   }
  309: 
  310:   /*
  311:    * Second preference:
  312:    * topic metadata on the original assignment.
  313:    */
  314:   const assignmentTopic =
  315:     assignmentMetadata?.topic ||
  316:     "";
  317: 
  318:   if (
  319:     assignmentTopic &&
  320:     !isGenericAssessmentTopic(
  321:       assignmentTopic,
  322:     )
  323:   ) {
  324:     return cleanAssignmentTitle(
  325:       assignmentTopic,
  326:     );
  327:   }
  328: 
  329:   /*
  330:    * Third preference:
  331:    * derive the curriculum topic from the original assignment
  332:    * title.
  333:    *
  334:    * Example:
  335:    * "character encoding Demo Quiz"
  336:    *          ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬Å“
  337:    * "character encoding"
  338:    *          ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬Å“
  339:    * topicNormalisationService
  340:    */
  341:   const assignmentTitle =
  342:     cleanAssignmentTitle(
  343:       assignmentMetadata?.title ||
  344:         "",
  345:     );
  346: 
  347:   if (
  348:     assignmentTitle &&
  349:     !isGenericAssessmentTopic(
  350:       assignmentTitle,
  351:     )
  352:   ) {
  353:     return assignmentTitle;
  354:   }
  355: 
  356:   /*
  357:    * Do NOT create fake mastery evidence such as "Assigned Quiz".
  358:    *
  359:    * The result remains stored normally in Firestore and can still
  360:    * contribute to assignment history/markbooks. It simply cannot
  361:    * be attributed to curriculum mastery until a real topic can be
  362:    * identified.
  363:    */
  364:   return null;
  365: }
  366: 
  367: export async function getAdaptiveLearningPlan(
  368:   studentId: string,
  369: ): Promise<AdaptiveLearningPlan> {
  370:   const id =
  371:     studentId.trim();
  372: 
  373:   if (!id) {
  374:     throw new Error(
  375:       "A valid student account is required.",
  376:     );
  377:   }
  378: 
  379:   const [
  380:     profile,
  381:     quizSnapshot,
  382:     assignedQuizSnapshot,
  383:     examAssignments,
  384:     interventions,
  385:   ] = await Promise.all([
  386:     getUserProfile(id),
  387: 
  388:     getDocs(
  389:       query(
  390:         collection(
  391:           db,
  392:           "users",
  393:           id,
  394:           "quizResults",
  395:         ),
  396:         orderBy(
  397:           "createdAt",
  398:           "desc",
  399:         ),
  400:       ),
  401:     ),
  402: 
  403:     getDocs(
  404:       query(
  405:         collection(
  406:           db,
  407:           "assignmentResults",
  408:         ),
  409:         where(
  410:           "studentId",
  411:           "==",
  412:           id,
  413:         ),
  414:       ),
  415:     ),
  416: 
  417:     getStudentExamAssignments(
  418:       id,
  419:     ),
  420: 
  421:     getStudentInterventions(
  422:       id,
  423:     ),
  424:   ]);
  425: 
  426:   if (!profile) {
  427:     throw new Error(
  428:       "The student profile could not be loaded.",
  429:     );
  430:   }
  431: 
  432:   const evidence:
  433:     AdaptiveEvidence[] = [];
  434: 
  435:   /*
  436:    * ------------------------------------------------------------
  437:    * NORMAL QUIZ EVIDENCE
  438:    * ------------------------------------------------------------
  439:    */
  440:   quizSnapshot.docs.forEach(
  441:     (document) => {
  442:       const data =
  443:         document.data();
  444: 
  445:       const topic =
  446:         resolveRawTopic(
  447:           data,
  448:           "Quiz",
  449:         );
  450: 
  451:       /*
  452:        * Avoid producing a generic "Quiz" mastery topic.
  453:        */
  454:       if (
  455:         isGenericAssessmentTopic(
  456:           topic,
  457:         )
  458:       ) {
  459:         return;
  460:       }
  461: 
  462:       evidence.push({
  463:         id:
  464:           `quiz-${document.id}`,
  465: 
  466:         topic:
  467:           cleanAssignmentTitle(
  468:             topic,
  469:           ),
  470: 
  471:         source: "quiz",
  472: 
  473:         score:
  474:           safeNumber(
  475:             data.scorePercent,
  476:           ),
  477: 
  478:         completedAt:
  479:           data.createdAt instanceof
  480:           Timestamp
  481:             ? data.createdAt.toDate()
  482:             : null,
  483: 
  484:         weight: 1,
  485:       });
  486:     },
  487:   );
  488: 
  489:   /*
  490:    * ------------------------------------------------------------
  491:    * ASSIGNED QUIZ EVIDENCE
  492:    * ------------------------------------------------------------
  493:    *
  494:    * assignmentResults often contain score/result information but
  495:    * not enough curriculum metadata. Resolve the original assignment
  496:    * so its real topic/title can be used.
  497:    */
  498:   const assignedQuizAssignmentIds =
  499:     assignedQuizSnapshot.docs
  500:       .map((document) =>
  501:         safeString(
  502:           document.data()
  503:             .assignmentId,
  504:         ),
  505:       )
  506:       .filter(Boolean);
  507: 
  508:   const assignmentMetadata =
  509:     await loadAssignmentTopicMetadata(
  510:       assignedQuizAssignmentIds,
  511:     );
  512: 
  513:   assignedQuizSnapshot.docs.forEach(
  514:     (document) => {
  515:       const data =
  516:         document.data();
  517: 
  518:       const assignmentId =
  519:         safeString(
  520:           data.assignmentId,
  521:         );
  522: 
  523:       const topic =
  524:         resolveAssignedQuizTopic({
  525:           resultData: data,
  526: 
  527:           assignmentMetadata:
  528:             assignmentMetadata.get(
  529:               assignmentId,
  530:             ) || null,
  531:         });
  532: 
  533:       if (!topic) {
  534:         console.warn(
  535:           `Assigned quiz result ${document.id} has no resolvable curriculum topic and was excluded from topic mastery.`,
  536:         );
  537: 
  538:         return;
  539:       }
  540: 
  541:       evidence.push({
  542:         id:
  543:           `assigned-quiz-${document.id}`,
  544: 
  545:         topic,
  546: 
  547:         source: "quiz",
  548: 
  549:         score:
  550:           safeNumber(
  551:             data.percentage,
  552:           ),
  553: 
  554:         completedAt:
  555:           data.completedAt instanceof
  556:           Timestamp
  557:             ? data.completedAt.toDate()
  558:             : null,
  559: 
  560:         weight: 1.1,
  561:       });
  562:     },
  563:   );
  564: 
  565:   /*
  566:    * ------------------------------------------------------------
  567:    * WRITTEN EXAM EVIDENCE
  568:    * ------------------------------------------------------------
  569:    */
  570:   const submissions =
  571:     await Promise.all(
  572:       examAssignments.map(
  573:         (assignment) =>
  574:           getExamSubmission(
  575:             assignment.id,
  576:             id,
  577:           ),
  578:       ),
  579:     );
  580: 
  581:   examAssignments.forEach(
  582:     (
  583:       assignment,
  584:       index,
  585:     ) => {
  586:       const submission =
  587:         submissions[index];
  588: 
  589:       if (
  590:         !submission ||
  591:         submission.status !==
  592:           "marked"
  593:       ) {
  594:         return;
  595:       }
  596: 
  597:       const examTopic =
  598:         assignment
  599:           .questionSetSnapshot
  600:           .topic ||
  601:         assignment
  602:           .questionSetTitle ||
  603:         assignment.title;
  604: 
  605:       if (
  606:         isGenericAssessmentTopic(
  607:           examTopic,
  608:         )
  609:       ) {
  610:         return;
  611:       }
  612: 
  613:       evidence.push({
  614:         id:
  615:           `exam-${assignment.id}`,
  616: 
  617:         topic:
  618:           cleanAssignmentTitle(
  619:             examTopic,
  620:           ),
  621: 
  622:         source: "exam",
  623: 
  624:         score:
  625:           submission.percentage,
  626: 
  627:         completedAt:
  628:           submission.markedAt,
  629: 
  630:         weight: 1.4,
  631:       });
  632:     },
  633:   );
  634: 
  635:   /*
  636:    * ------------------------------------------------------------
  637:    * INTERVENTION EVIDENCE
  638:    * ------------------------------------------------------------
  639:    */
  640:   interventions.forEach(
  641:     (intervention) => {
  642:       const completedSteps =
  643:         intervention.steps.filter(
  644:           (step) =>
  645:             step.status ===
  646:             "completed",
  647:         ).length;
  648: 
  649:       if (!completedSteps) {
  650:         return;
  651:       }
  652: 
  653:       if (
  654:         isGenericAssessmentTopic(
  655:           intervention.topic,
  656:         )
  657:       ) {
  658:         return;
  659:       }
  660: 
  661:       evidence.push({
  662:         id:
  663:           `intervention-${intervention.id}`,
  664: 
  665:         topic:
  666:           intervention.topic,
  667: 
  668:         source:
  669:           "intervention",
  670: 
  671:         score:
  672:           intervention.currentScore ||
  673:           intervention.baselineScore,
  674: 
  675:         completedAt:
  676:           intervention.updatedAt ||
  677:           intervention.completedAt,
  678: 
  679:         weight: 0.8,
  680:       });
  681:     },
  682:   );
  683: 
  684:   /*
  685:    * ------------------------------------------------------------
  686:    * LESSON EVIDENCE
  687:    * ------------------------------------------------------------
  688:    */
  689:   profile.completedLessons.forEach(
  690:     (
  691:       lessonId,
  692:       index,
  693:     ) => {
  694:       if (
  695:         !lessonId.trim()
  696:       ) {
  697:         return;
  698:       }
  699: 
  700:       evidence.push({
  701:         id:
  702:           `lesson-${index}-${lessonId}`,
  703: 
  704:         topic: lessonId,
  705: 
  706:         source: "lesson",
  707: 
  708:         score: 60,
  709: 
  710:         completedAt: null,
  711: 
  712:         weight: 0.25,
  713:       });
  714:     },
  715:   );
  716: 
  717:   /*
  718:    * ------------------------------------------------------------
  719:    * MASTERY + RECOMMENDATIONS
  720:    * ------------------------------------------------------------
  721:    */
  722:   const topics =
  723:     buildTopicMastery(
  724:       groupEvidence(
  725:         evidence,
  726:       ),
  727:     );
  728: 
  729:   const actions =
  730:     buildAdaptiveActions(
  731:       topics,
  732:     );
  733: 
  734:   const assessedTopics =
  735:     topics.filter(
  736:       (topic) =>
  737:         topic.attempts > 0,
  738:     );
  739: 
  740:   const overallMastery =
  741:     average(
  742:       assessedTopics.map(
  743:         (topic) =>
  744:           topic.masteryScore,
  745:       ),
  746:     );
  747: 
  748:   const confidence =
  749:     average(
  750:       assessedTopics.map(
  751:         (topic) =>
  752:           topic.confidenceScore,
  753:       ),
  754:     );
  755: 
  756:   const examReadiness =
  757:     average(
  758:       assessedTopics.map(
  759:         (topic) =>
  760:           Math.round(
  761:             topic.masteryScore *
  762:               0.75 +
  763:               topic.confidenceScore *
  764:                 0.25,
  765:           ),
  766:       ),
  767:     );
  768: 
  769:   const recentTrend =
  770:     average(
  771:       assessedTopics.map(
  772:         (topic) =>
  773:           topic.trend,
  774:       ),
  775:     );
  776: 
  777:   const predictedScore =
  778:     Math.max(
  779:       0,
  780:       Math.min(
  781:         100,
  782:         examReadiness +
  783:           Math.round(
  784:             recentTrend *
  785:               0.25,
  786:           ),
  787:       ),
  788:     );
  789: 
  790:   return {
  791:     studentId: id,
  792: 
  793:     generatedAt:
  794:       new Date(),
  795: 
  796:     overallMastery,
  797: 
  798:     examReadiness,
  799: 
  800:     confidence,
  801: 
  802:     currentGrade:
  803:       indicativeGradeFromPercentage(
  804:         examReadiness,
  805:         profile?.qualification,
  806:       ),
  807: 
  808:     predictedGrade:
  809:       indicativeGradeFromPercentage(
  810:         predictedScore,
  811:         profile?.qualification,
  812:       ),
  813: 
  814:     dueForReviewCount:
  815:       topics.filter(
  816:         (topic) =>
  817:           topic.nextReviewAt.getTime() <=
  818:           Date.now(),
  819:       ).length,
  820: 
  821:     priorityTopicCount:
  822:       topics.filter(
  823:         (topic) =>
  824:           topic.state ===
  825:             "priority" ||
  826:           topic.state ===
  827:             "forgetting-risk",
  828:       ).length,
  829: 
  830:     secureTopicCount:
  831:       topics.filter(
  832:         (topic) =>
  833:           topic.state ===
  834:             "secure" ||
  835:           topic.state ===
  836:             "mastered",
  837:       ).length,
  838: 
  839:     nextAction:
  840:       actions[0] || null,
  841: 
  842:     actions,
  843: 
  844:     topics,
  845:   };
  846: }

==============================================================================================================
FILE: services\adaptiveRecommendationService.ts
==============================================================================================================
    1: import { curriculumRegistry } from "@/data/curriculum/curriculumRegistry";
    2: 
    3: import type {
    4:   AdaptiveLearningAction,
    5:   AdaptiveTopicMastery,
    6: } from "@/types/adaptiveLearning";
    7: 
    8: const encoded = (value: string) =>
    9:   encodeURIComponent(value);
   10: 
   11: function normaliseLookupValue(
   12:   value: string,
   13: ): string {
   14:   return value
   15:     .trim()
   16:     .toLowerCase()
   17:     .replace(/&/g, "and")
   18:     .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, " ")
   19:     .trim();
   20: }
   21: 
   22: function resolveCurriculumTopicId(
   23:   topic: AdaptiveTopicMastery,
   24: ): string | null {
   25:   const candidates = [
   26:     topic.topic,
   27:     topic.id,
   28:   ]
   29:     .map(normaliseLookupValue)
   30:     .filter(Boolean);
   31: 
   32:   if (candidates.length === 0) {
   33:     return null;
   34:   }
   35: 
   36:   const match =
   37:     curriculumRegistry.find(
   38:       (curriculumTopic) => {
   39:         const searchableValues = [
   40:           curriculumTopic.id,
   41:           curriculumTopic.title,
   42:           ...(curriculumTopic.aliases || []),
   43:         ].map(normaliseLookupValue);
   44: 
   45:         return candidates.some(
   46:           (candidate) =>
   47:             searchableValues.includes(
   48:               candidate,
   49:             ),
   50:         );
   51:       },
   52:     );
   53: 
   54:   return match?.id || null;
   55: }
   56: 
   57: function learningHref(
   58:   topic: AdaptiveTopicMastery,
   59: ): string {
   60:   const curriculumTopicId =
   61:     resolveCurriculumTopicId(topic);
   62: 
   63:   /*
   64:    * Preferred route:
   65:    * resolve the adaptive topic through the curriculum registry.
   66:    *
   67:    * Example:
   68:    * Character Encoding
   69:    *     â†“
   70:    * curriculumRegistry alias/title match
   71:    *     â†“
   72:    * id: "characters"
   73:    *     â†“
   74:    * /learn/characters
   75:    */
   76:   if (curriculumTopicId) {
   77:     return `/learn/${encoded(
   78:       curriculumTopicId,
   79:     )}`;
   80:   }
   81: 
   82:   /*
   83:    * Defensive fallback.
   84:    *
   85:    * If a topic has not yet been registered in the curriculum,
   86:    * return to the searchable Learn catalogue rather than creating
   87:    * a broken /learn/[topicId] route.
   88:    */
   89:   return `/learn?search=${encoded(
   90:     topic.topic,
   91:   )}`;
   92: }
   93: 
   94: export function buildAdaptiveActions(
   95:   topics: AdaptiveTopicMastery[],
   96: ): AdaptiveLearningAction[] {
   97:   const actions: AdaptiveLearningAction[] =
   98:     [];
   99: 
  100:   topics
  101:     .filter(
  102:       (topic) =>
  103:         topic.state !== "mastered",
  104:     )
  105:     .slice(0, 3)
  106:     .forEach((topic, index) => {
  107:       const priority: AdaptiveLearningAction["priority"] =
  108:         topic.priorityScore >= 70
  109:           ? "high"
  110:           : topic.priorityScore >= 45
  111:             ? "medium"
  112:             : "low";
  113: 
  114:       /*
  115:        * New or weak topics should take the learner directly
  116:        * into the corresponding curriculum topic wherever one
  117:        * exists.
  118:        */
  119:       if (
  120:         topic.state === "new" ||
  121:         topic.masteryScore < 45
  122:       ) {
  123:         actions.push({
  124:           id: `lesson-${topic.id}`,
  125:           type: "lesson",
  126:           title: `Review ${topic.topic}`,
  127:           description: topic.reason,
  128:           topic: topic.topic,
  129:           href: learningHref(topic),
  130:           estimatedMinutes: 12,
  131:           xpReward: 25,
  132:           priority,
  133:         });
  134:       }
  135: 
  136:       /*
  137:        * Retrieval practice remains topic-specific.
  138:        */
  139:       actions.push({
  140:         id: `quiz-${topic.id}`,
  141:         type: "quiz",
  142:         title:
  143:           `Retrieval quiz: ${topic.topic}`,
  144:         description:
  145:           `Use ${topic.recommendedDifficulty} difficulty to check recall and update mastery.`,
  146:         topic: topic.topic,
  147:         href:
  148:           `/quiz?topic=${encoded(
  149:             topic.topic,
  150:           )}&difficulty=${encoded(
  151:             topic.recommendedDifficulty,
  152:           )}`,
  153:         estimatedMinutes: 8,
  154:         xpReward: 30,
  155:         priority,
  156:       });
  157: 
  158:       /*
  159:        * Written exam practice remains assignment-driven.
  160:        * The student can only attempt papers that have actually
  161:        * been assigned to them.
  162:        */
  163:       if (
  164:         index === 0 ||
  165:         topic.masteryScore >= 40
  166:       ) {
  167:         actions.push({
  168:           id: `exam-${topic.id}`,
  169:           type: "exam",
  170:           title:
  171:             `Exam practice: ${topic.topic}`,
  172:           description:
  173:             "Apply knowledge in a written response and compare it with the mark scheme.",
  174:           topic: topic.topic,
  175:           href:
  176:             "/assignments?filter=exams",
  177:           estimatedMinutes: 12,
  178:           xpReward: 40,
  179:           priority:
  180:             priority === "high"
  181:               ? "high"
  182:               : "medium",
  183:         });
  184:       }
  185:     });
  186: 
  187:   return actions.slice(0, 6);
  188: }

==============================================================================================================
FILE: services\curriculumCoverageService.ts
==============================================================================================================
    1: import {
    2:   getCurriculumDefinition,
    3:   type CurriculumDefinition,
    4:   type CurriculumUnitDefinition,
    5: } from "@/data/curriculum/curriculumMap";
    6: import { topicLibrary } from "@/data/curriculum/topics";
    7: import type { Topic } from "@/types/curriculum";
    8: import type { ExamBoard, Qualification } from "@/types/user";
    9: 
   10: export type CurriculumUnitCoverage = {
   11:   unit: CurriculumUnitDefinition;
   12:   topics: Topic[];
   13:   missingTopicIds: string[];
   14:   lessonCount: number;
   15: };
   16: 
   17: export type CurriculumCoverage = {
   18:   curriculum: CurriculumDefinition;
   19:   units: CurriculumUnitCoverage[];
   20:   mappedTopicCount: number;
   21:   availableTopicCount: number;
   22:   lessonCount: number;
   23:   missingTopicIds: string[];
   24:   complete: boolean;
   25: };
   26: 
   27: export function getCurriculumCoverage(
   28:   qualification: Qualification,
   29:   examBoard: ExamBoard,
   30: ): CurriculumCoverage | null {
   31:   const curriculum =
   32:     getCurriculumDefinition(
   33:       qualification,
   34:       examBoard,
   35:     );
   36: 
   37:   if (!curriculum) {
   38:     return null;
   39:   }
   40: 
   41:   const units: CurriculumUnitCoverage[] =
   42:     curriculum.units.map((unit) => {
   43:       const topics = unit.topicIds
   44:         .map((topicId) =>
   45:           topicLibrary[topicId],
   46:         )
   47:         .filter(
   48:           (topic): topic is Topic =>
   49:             Boolean(topic) &&
   50:             topic.status !== "coming-soon" &&
   51:             topic.lessons.length > 0,
   52:         );
   53: 
   54:       const missingTopicIds =
   55:         unit.topicIds.filter(
   56:           (topicId) =>
   57:             !topicLibrary[topicId] ||
   58:             topicLibrary[topicId].status ===
   59:               "coming-soon" ||
   60:             topicLibrary[topicId].lessons.length === 0,
   61:         );
   62: 
   63:       return {
   64:         unit,
   65:         topics,
   66:         missingTopicIds,
   67:         lessonCount: topics.reduce(
   68:           (total, topic) =>
   69:             total + topic.lessons.length,
   70:           0,
   71:         ),
   72:       };
   73:     });
   74: 
   75:   const mappedTopicIds = Array.from(
   76:     new Set(
   77:       curriculum.units.flatMap(
   78:         (unit) => unit.topicIds,
   79:       ),
   80:     ),
   81:   );
   82: 
   83:   const availableTopicIds = Array.from(
   84:     new Set(
   85:       units.flatMap((unit) =>
   86:         unit.topics.map(
   87:           (topic) => topic.id,
   88:         ),
   89:       ),
   90:     ),
   91:   );
   92: 
   93:   const missingTopicIds = Array.from(
   94:     new Set(
   95:       units.flatMap(
   96:         (unit) =>
   97:           unit.missingTopicIds,
   98:       ),
   99:     ),
  100:   );
  101: 
  102:   return {
  103:     curriculum,
  104:     units,
  105:     mappedTopicCount:
  106:       mappedTopicIds.length,
  107:     availableTopicCount:
  108:       availableTopicIds.length,
  109:     lessonCount: units.reduce(
  110:       (total, unit) =>
  111:         total + unit.lessonCount,
  112:       0,
  113:     ),
  114:     missingTopicIds,
  115:     complete:
  116:       missingTopicIds.length === 0,
  117:   };
  118: }

    1: import {
    2:   addDoc,

   16: import { db } from "@/lib/firebase";
   17: import {
   18:   DEFAULT_EXAM_INTEGRITY_POLICY,
   19:   type CreateExamAssignmentInput,
   20:   type ExamAssignment,
   21:   type ExamAssignmentStatus,
   22:   type ExamIntegrityPolicy,
   23:   type ExamVisibilityAction,
